```
def smooth_obj_grad(h, n, alpha):
  c = correlate * dx
  m = c.max()
  z = alpha*(c-m)
  z -= z.max() maybe (m=0? c-m <=0, max0). sh=z; p=np.exp(sh); p/=p.sum()
  # G = m + (1/alpha) log(sum p) (since p sum). Actually logsumexp z = max z + log(sum exp(z-maxz)). max z=0. G=m + log(psum)/alpha.
  grad=zeros
  active = p > 1e-8 maybe all.
  for idx,pd in enumerate(p): if pd>eps: add gradient for lag = idx-(n-1)
  return G, grad
```

```
import numpy as np, scipy.optimize, time, math

def exact_c5(h,n):
    dx=2.0/n
    c=np.correlate(h, 1.0-h, mode='full')*dx
    return float(np.max(c))
```

```
def project_vector(v,n):
 target=n/2
 v=np.asarray(v,float)
 lo=np.min(v)-1.0
 hi=np.max(v)+1.0
 for _ in range(60):
   mid=(lo+hi)/2
   s=np.clip(v-mid,0,1).sum()
   if s>target: lo=mid
   else: hi=mid
 h=np.clip(v-mid,0,1)
 # adjust rounding residual
 diff=target-h.sum()
 if abs(diff)>1e-12:
    # find free indices (0<h<1-1e-12)
    free=(h>1e-12)&(h<1-1e-12)
    if free.any():
       h[free]+=diff/free.sum()
       h=np.clip(h,0,1)
       # residual due clip? maybe do simple distribute to non-saturated one direction
       while abs(h.sum()-target)>1e-10:
          diff=target-h.sum()
          if diff>0:
             idx=np.argmax(1-h)
          else:
             idx=np.argmin(h)
          if 0<h[idx]<1:
             step=min(abs(diff), abs(1-h[idx]) if diff<0 else h[idx])
             h[idx]+= diff if diff<0? Actually if sum low, add positive; step = min(diff, 1-h[idx]); h[idx]+=step else subtract min(-diff,h[idx])
             diff=target-h.sum()
          else: break
 return h
```
But bisection for each projection (many) may be costly. We can implement fast approximate: majority of h already feasible; do clip, then adjust by scaling free/ bounds as parent. For SLSQP start, use parent project. For final, use robust. Use `project_feasible_fast` with iterative room scaling 100. It is O(iter*n), fine. Bisection 60*n for many projections maybe heavy. Use scaling method:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
 target=n/2
 h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 for _ in range(1000):
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
   if diff>0:
      room=1-h
      s=room.sum()
      if s<1e-14: break
      frac=diff/s
      h=np.minimum(1,h+frac*room)
   else:
      s=h.sum()
      if s<1e-14: break
      # remove proportionally but respect 0
      h=np.maximum(0,h+ diff*(h/s))
 return np.clip(h,0,1)
```
This may leave diff if saturate; loop handles. At end, if diff not zero, distribute to free. Parent accepted. We'll add exact final adjust.

```
def smooth_g_and_weights(c, alpha): ...
def smooth_objective_grad_x(h, n, alpha):
    c=np.correlate(h,1-h,2*n-1)*dx  # maybe use np.correlate
    m=c.max()
    if m<=0: weights uniform? G=0? grad zeros
    z=alpha*(c-m)
    # z max 0; subtract max for stability
    z=z-z.max()  # z max 0, z<=0; but subtracting doesn't change p? If z max 0 already, yes.
    e=np.exp(z)
    p=e/e.sum()
    G=m + float(np.log(e.sum()))/alpha
    grad=zeros
    tol=1e-10
    for idx,pd in enumerate(p):
       if pd<tol: continue
       d=idx-(n-1)
       add grad_d with pd
    return G, grad
```

Active-set algorithm:
```
def active_slsqp(h, n, time_limit, tol_options):
  best_h=h; best_c=exact_c5
  for round in range(max_rounds):
    if time>limit: break
    c_vals=correlate* dx; m=max
    # choose active: top indices where c_vals > m - active_tol. active_tol maybe sequence [1e-3,1e-4,1e-5]
    idx=np.where(c_vals > m - btol)[0]
    if len(idx)==0: idx=[argmax]
```

`add_lag_grad` top-level:
```
def add_lag_grad(grad,d,p,h,n):
    if n<=0: return
    if abs(d)<0? :
       grad[:] += p
       grad[:] -= 2.0*p*h
    elif d>0:
       L=n-d
       if L<=0: return
       grad[:L] += p
       grad[:L] -= p*h[d:d+L]
       grad[d:] -= p*h[:L]
    else:
       e=-d
       L=n-e
       if L<=0: return
       grad[e:] += p
       grad[e:] -= p*h[:L]
       grad[:L] -= p*h[e:]
```
Here `p` already includes weight*dx? For active mean, weight = dx / count. For smooth, weight = prob*dx.

`active_objective(x,n,idx,k)`:
```
def active_objective(x,n,idx,k):
    x=np.asarray(x)
    dx=2.0/n
    total=0.0
    for d in (idx - (n-1)):
      if d==0:
        total += np.sum(x*(1-x)) # okay
      elif d>0:
        L=n-d
        if L>0: total += np.sum(x[:L]*(1-x[d:]))
      else:
        e=-d; L=n-e
        if L>0: total += np.sum(x[e:]* (1-x[:L]))
    return (total/k)*dx
```
Need if idx length zero return 0. k positive.

Let's implement direct active gradient carefully for speed: local variables, avoid repeated `n-d` maybe.
```
def active_gradient(x,n,idx,k):
    grad=np.zeros(n)
    w=(2.0/n)/k
    for l in idx:
       d=int(l-(n-1))
       if d==0:
          grad += w
          grad -= 2*w*x
       elif d>0:
          L=n-d
          if L>0:
             grad[:L] += w
             grad[:L] -= w*x[d:]
             grad[d:] -= w*x[:L]
       else:
          e=-d; L=n-e
          if L>0:
             grad[e:] += w
             grad[e:] -= w*x[:L]
             grad[:L] -= w*x[e:]
    return grad
```
For d>0, `x[d:]` length L; `x[:L]`. For d<0, `x[:L]` length L, `x[e:]`.

```
def smooth_objective(x,n,alpha):
  g=full_g_values(x,n)
  m=g.max()
  # If all zero? p uniform; G=m + log(num)/alpha
  z=alpha*(g-m); z-=z.max() # z max 0
  e=np.exp(z); s=e.sum()
  return m + np.log(s)/alpha

def smooth_gradient(x,n,alpha):
  g=full_g_values(x,n)
  m=g.max(); z=alpha*(g-m)-max...; e; p=e/s
  grad=zeros
  # skip p<1e-9
  for i,pd in enumerate(p):
    if pd>1e-9: add_lag_grad(grad,d,pd*(2.0/n),x,n)
  return grad
```

Now exact objective after projection: Because project_feasible may slightly change h and sum; recompute. For final, ensure sum residual <1e-9. We'll implement `strict_project` final with direct adjustment.

Feasibility: `sum(h)*dx=1`. We can return h with floating sum exactly target? We can adjust last free entry.
```
def enforce_sum_exact(h,n):
 target=n/2.0
 h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 diff=target-h.sum()
 if abs(diff)>0:
   # if diff positive, find indices not at 1, add proportionally; else not at 0
   if diff>0:
      mask=h<1-1e-15
   else:
      mask=h>1e-15
   if mask.any():
      h[mask]+=diff/mask.sum()
      h=np.clip(h,0,1)
   # if still residual, distribute one by one
   for _ in range(100):
      diff=target-h.sum()
      if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
      if diff>0:
         idx=int(np.argmax(1-h))
         step=min(diff,1-h[idx])
         h[idx]+=step
      else:
         idx=int(np.argmin(h))
         step=min(-diff,h[idx])
         h[idx]-=step
 return h
```
Use as final. In SLSQP, use faster project (scaling) to avoid overhead.

```
def make_tent(n, center_high=True):
  x=np.linspace(0,2,n)
  f=1-2*np.abs(x-1) if center_high else 2*np.abs(x-1)-1
  h=0.5*(1+f)
  return project_feasible(h,n)
def make_random_smooth(n,seed, K=5):
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  x=np.linspace(0,2,n); y=x-1
  f=np.zeros(n)
  for k in range(1,K+1):
    f += rng.normal(0,1.0*math.exp(-0.5*(k-1)*0.3))*np.cos(k*np.pi*y)
  # ensure sum zero? project h
  h=0.5*(1+f)
  return project_feasible(h,n)
```

```
def choose_active_indices(vals, maxK, tol):
    m=vals.max()
    idx=np.where(vals >= m - tol)[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
       idx=np.array([vals.argmax()])
    if idx.size > maxK:
       # take top maxK by value
       idx=idx[np.argsort(vals[idx])[::-1][:maxK]]  # sorting local
    # ensure 1D int
    return idx
```

```
def active_polish(h,n,deadline, max_rounds=200, maxK=400):
  best=...; stall=0; round=0
  while time<deadline and round<max_rounds:
    vals=full_g_values(best,n); m=vals.max()

    K = min(maxK, 10 * (1 << (round // 3)))  # 10,10,10,40...
    if K<10: K=10
    tol = max(1e-7, 1e-3 * (0.6 ** round))
    # Also if exact very close, tol smaller
    idx = choose_active(vals,K,tol)
    hstart = best

       hstart = project_feasible(best + 0.02*rng.randn(n),n)
    # SLSQP

       best=...; stall=0; vals=...? continue with same round? maybe round+=1
    else:

          p=project_feasible((1-f)*best + f*res.x,n); c=exact; if improve...
```

`active_objective` must handle dlist as numpy array. It computes total via loops. For speed, use local variables and if d>0 etc. If dlist empty return 0.
```
def active_objective(x,n,dlist,k):
    if k <= 0: return 0.0
    dx=2.0/n
    total=0.0
    for d in dlist:
       dd=int(d)
       if dd==0:
          total += float(np.dot(x,1.0-x)) # or sum
       elif dd>0:
          L=n-dd
          if L>0:
             total += float(np.dot(x[:L], 1.0-x[dd:dd+L]))
       else:
          e=-dd; L=n-e
          if L>0:
             total += float(np.dot(x[e:], 1.0-x[:L]))
    return (total*dx)/k
```
Using `np.dot(x[:L], 1.0-x[dd:])` creates 1-x temp. Could use `np.sum(x[:L]*(1.0-x[dd:dd+L]))`. Fine. `np.dot(x[:L], -x[dd:dd+L]) + sum(x[:L])` maybe faster? `np.dot(a,b)` with b=1-x temp. We can compute `L - np.dot(x[:L], x[dd:])`? For positive d: sum x_i - x_i x_{i+d}; total += `sum(x[:L]) - dot(x[:L], x[dd:])`. This avoids 1 temp. Need sum x[:L] each loop; can use x.sum? Not. Compute `s = x[:L].sum()`. That's another pass. Dot maybe faster. Use `float(np.dot(x[:L], 1.0 - x[dd:dd+L]))`; okay.
For d=0: sum x - dot(x,x). 
We can use `np.dot(x, 1.0-x)` or `x.sum()-dot(x,x)`. Use latter to avoid temp: `float(x.sum() - np.dot(x,x))`.
Positive: `float(x[:L].sum() - np.dot(x[:L], x[dd:dd+L]))`. Negative: `float(x[e:].sum() - np.dot(x[e:], x[:L]))`.
This is efficient.

Active gradient:
```
def active_gradient(x,n,dlist,k):
    grad=np.zeros(n,dtype=float)
    w=(2.0/n)/k
    for d in dlist:
       dd=int(d)
       if dd==0:
          grad[:] += w
          grad[:] -= 2.0*w*x
       elif dd>0:
          L=n-dd
          if L>0:
             grad[:L] += w
             grad[:L] -= w*x[dd:dd+L]
             grad[dd:] -= w*x[:L]
       else:
          e=-dd; L=n-e
          if L>0:
             grad[e:] += w
             grad[e:] -= w*x[:L]
             grad[:L] -= w*x[e:]
    return grad
```
Check d positive formula: For S_d=∑_{i=0}^{L-1} h_i(1-h_{i+d}). linear + on first L; product: -h_{i+d} => grad[i] for i first L subtract h_{i+d}; -h_i for second i=d.. subtract h_{i-d} => grad[d:] -= h[:L]. Good.

Smooth gradient uses weights p over d list all `d = (n-1)-idx`. We can build all d for n each call? To avoid allocate, loop over idx and compute d. `p = np.exp(z)/s`; for idx,pd in enumerate(p): if pd>1e-10: d=(n-1)-idx; add. Good. `z` length 2n-1, exp. For n=512, length1023; okay.

Smooth objective `g = np.dot?` use correlate. We need exact g values with dx. `np.correlate(x,1.0-x,'full')*dx`. This creates temp 1-x. Fine.

Project fast:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    target = n/2.0
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    np.clip(h,0,1,out=h)
    for _ in range(100):
       s=h.sum()
       diff=target-s
       if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
       if diff>0:
          room=1.0-h
          r=room.sum()
          if r<1e-14: break
          # distribute to room, but if diff > s? diff <=? no
          h = np.minimum(1.0, h + diff/room.sum()*room) # room.sum recomputed? use r
       else:
          s2=s
          if s2<1e-14: break
          h = np.maximum(0.0, h + diff*(h/s2))
    # final residual
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-12:
       if diff>0: mask=h<1-1e-14
       else: mask=h>1e-14
       if mask.any():
          h[mask]+=diff/mask.sum()
          h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    return h
```
This uses scaling for sum>target. If sum low, adding diff/r*room may saturate; loop 100.
For SLSQP, h often near target; fast.

Strict project bisection? For final, use same plus direct. Could be enough. If target n/2 and h all 0/1 with correct count, diff zero. If SLSQP returns slight infeasible, scaling might break box? It uses clip after.

Maybe scaling for diff>0 (need add mass) can exceed 1; we clip and loop. For diff<0, scaling down proportionally (not exactly subtract with 0 bound? `h + diff*(h/s)` where diff negative. If some h=0 remain0, okay; if h sorted, max clipped if diff too large? loop.

Active polish SLSQP: It can return h with sum not exactly target but within. Use project.

Let's draft code mentally.

```
import time
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_TARGET_SUM = 0.0

def _sum_constraint(x):
    return float(np.sum(x)) - _TARGET_SUM

def full_g_values(h,n):
    dx = 2.0 / n
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full') * dx

def exact_c5(h,n):
    return float(np.max(full_g_values(h,n)))
```

Project:
```
def project_feasible(h,n):
    target = 0.5*n
    h = np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    for _ in range(200):
        s = float(np.sum(h))
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13: break
        if diff > 0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            r = float(np.sum(room))
            if r < 1e-15: break
            h = np.minimum(1.0, h + (diff/r)*room)
        else:
            s2 = s
            if s2 < 1e-15: break
            h = np.maximum(0.0, h + (diff/s2)*h)
    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
       if diff > 0:
          mask = h < (1.0 - 1e-14)
       else:
          mask = h > 1e-14
       if np.any(mask):
          h[mask] += diff / float(np.sum(mask))
          np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    return h
```
If h all 0 and target>0, room sum n; add. If all 1 and target<n, scale down. Good.

Strict final:
```
def strict_project_feasible(h,n):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    target=0.5*n
    for _ in range(1000):
       diff=target-h.sum()
       if abs(diff)<1e-14: break
       if diff>0:
          idx=int(np.argmax(1.0-h))
          step=min(diff,1.0-h[idx])
          h[idx]+=step
       else:
          idx=int(np.argmin(h))
          step=min(-diff,h[idx])
          h[idx]-=step
    return h
```
But if h[idx] saturated due argmax? If no room, no change; loop. Use project after. Fine.

Choose active:
```
def choose_active_d(vals, maxK, tol):
    m = float(np.max(vals))
    idx = np.where(vals >= m - tol)[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
       idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(vals))])
    if idx.size > maxK:
       # top maxK
       # sort idx by vals descending
       order = np.argsort(vals[idx])[::-1]
       idx = idx[order[:maxK]]
    # convert to d lags
    d = (len(vals)//2) - idx  # n-1 = (len(vals)-1)/2
    return np.asarray(d, dtype=int)
```
Since len(vals)=2n-1, (len(vals)-1)//2. Use n passed maybe.

```
def choose_active_d(vals,n,maxK,tol):
   ... d = (n-1)-idx
```

Active functions:
```
def active_objective(x,n,dlist,k):
   if k<=0 or len(dlist)==0: return 0.0
   dx=2.0/n
   total=0.0
   x=np.asarray(x)
   for dd in dlist:
      d=int(dd)
      if d==0:
         total += float(np.sum(x) - np.dot(x,x))
      elif d>0:
         L=n-d
         if L>0:
            total += float(np.sum(x[:L]) - np.dot(x[:L], x[d:d+L]))
      else:
         e=-d; L=n-e
         if L>0:
            total += float(np.sum(x[e:]) - np.dot(x[e:], x[:L]))
   return (total*dx)/k
```
For d=0, if x sum maybe not target, but objective c0 includes dx. Good.

```
def smooth_objective(x,n,alpha):
   g=full_g_values(x,n)
   m=float(np.max(g))
   if alpha <=0: return m
   z=alpha*(g-m)
   zmax=float(np.max(z))
   z=z-zmax
   e=np.exp(z)
   s=float(np.sum(e))
   return m + (zmax + np.log(s))/alpha  # Since logsumexp(original z)=zmax+log(s). But original z max 0, zmax originally 0. If subtract zmax, add back.
```
Actually z=alpha*(g-m); max z=0. If zmax=0, G=m+log(s)/alpha. Use general formula.

SLSQP runs:
```
def run_slsqp(func, x0, jac, args, n, maxiter):
    global _TARGET_SUM
    _TARGET_SUM = 0.5*n
    bounds = [(0.0,1.0)]*n
    cons = [{'type':'eq','fun':_sum_constraint}]
    try:
       res = minimize(func, x0, method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds, constraints=cons,
                      jac=jac, args=args, options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-12})
       return np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
    except Exception:
       return x0
```
No closure. This function top-level. But `args` includes arrays. Good. `maxiter` int.

Potential: SLSQP may call objective with x slightly outside bounds starting? x0 inside.

Local polish:
```
def local_polish(h,n,deadline,rng):
   h=project_feasible(h,n); best_c=exact_c5(h,n)
   step=0.02
   it=0
   while time.time() < deadline and it < 3000:
      it+=1
      x=h.copy()
      if rng.rand() < 0.5:
         i=int(rng.randint(n)); j=int(rng.randint(n))
         if i==j: continue
         delta=step*(2*rng.rand()-1)
         ni=x[i]+delta; nj=x[j]-delta
         if ni<0 or ni>1 or nj<0 or nj>1: 
            # try clip? skip
            step*=1.05 if step<0.1 else 0.95? maybe
            continue
         x[i]=ni; x[j]=nj
      else:
         noise=rng.normal(0.0, step, n)
         noise -= noise.mean()
         x=np.clip(h + noise,0,1)
         x=project_feasible(x,n)
      c=exact_c5(x,n)
      if c < best_c - 1e-13:
         h=x; best_c=c; step=min(0.1, step*1.2)
      else:
         step=max(0.002, step*0.98)
   return h,best_c
```
3000 iters at n=250 maybe okay. For n=512, 1000 iters. Set max_iters based on n: `max_iters = 4000 if n<=300 else 1500`.

```
def make_random_start(n, seed):
  rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
  y=2.0*np.arange(n)/(n-1)-1.0 if n>1 else np.zeros(n)
  f=np.zeros(n)
  for k in range(1,6):
     amp = math.exp(-0.25*(k-1))
     f += rng.normal(0,amp)*np.cos(k*np.pi*y)
  # add tent 0.5
  f += 0.7*(1.0-2.0*np.abs(y))
  return project_feasible(0.5*(1.0+f),n)
```

Pseudo code:
```
def active_polish(h,n,deadline,rng):
    h=project_feasible(h,n)
    best_c=exact_c5(h,n)
    maxK = max(16, min(320, 30 + n//2))
    Ks=[]; kvals=[8,16,32,64,128,256]
    for K in kvals:
       if K>2*n-2: K=2*n-2
       if K not in Ks and len(Ks)<7: Ks.append(K)
    touts=[1e-3,3e-4,1e-4,3e-5,1e-5,3e-6]
    max_calls = 70 if n<=350 else 35
    calls=0
    last_improved=False
    for K in Ks:
       for tol in touts:
          if time.time() >= deadline - 20: return h,best_c
          improved_cycle=False
          subs=0
          while subs<3 and calls<max_calls and time.time() < deadline - 25:
             vals=full_g_values(h,n)
             dlist=choose_active_d(vals,n,min(K, len(vals)-1), max(tol, 1e-7?))
             if dlist.size==0: break
             hstart=h
             if subs>0:
                hstart=project_feasible(h + (0.01+0.02*subs)*rng.randn(n), n)
             mi = 500 if K<=16 else 350 if K<=64 else 220 if K<=128 else 140
             # if left small reduce
             left=deadline-time.time()
             if left < 60: mi=min(mi, int(max(50, left)))
             res = run_slsqp(active_objective,hstart,active_gradient,(n,dlist,dlist.size),n,mi)
             calls +=1
             # candidates
             cand=res
             p, c, ok = try_candidate(cand,n,h,best_c)
             if ok:
                 h=p; best_c=c; improved_cycle=True; subs+=1; continue
             # try interpolations
             for f in (0.5,0.25,0.75):
                 cand = h + f*(res-h)
                 p,c,ok=try_candidate(cand,n,h,best_c)
                 if ok: ...; break
             if not ok:
                 # if subs>0 break to next tol; if subs==0 try less noise? break
                 break
          if improved_cycle:
             # continue next tol? yes
             continue
    return h,best_c
```

```
def smooth_polish(h,n,deadline,rng):
  h=project; best_c=exact
  alphas=[100,300,1000]
  max_calls=10
  for alpha in alphas:
    for attempt in range(2):
       if time > deadline-30 or calls>=max_calls: return
       hstart=h if attempt==0 else project(h + 0.03*rng.randn(n))
       mi=300 if alpha<=300 else 200
       res=run_slsqp(smooth_objective,hstart,smooth_gradient,(n,alpha),n,mi)
       calls+=1
       candidates res and interp; if improve update.
  return
```

`run_slsqp` function:
```
def run_slsqp(func, x0, jac, args, n, maxiter):
    global _TARGET_SUM
    _TARGET_SUM = 0.5 * float(n)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * int(n)
    cons = [{'type': 'eq', 'fun': _sum_constraint}]
    try:
        res = minimize(func, np.asarray(x0, dtype=float), method='SLSQP', bounds=bounds,
                       constraints=cons, jac=jac, args=args,
                       options={'maxiter': int(maxiter), 'ftol': 1e-12})
        return np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
    except Exception:
        return np.asarray(x0, dtype=float).copy()
```
If `args` is tuple, for active_gradient, okay. If `jac` signature active_gradient(x,n,dlist,k). Yes.

```
    if c < best_c - 1e-14:
       return p,c,True
    return best_h,best_c,False
```

```python
import time
import math
import numpy as np
from scipy.optimize import minimize

_TARGET_SUM = 0.0


def _sum_constraint(x):
    return float(np.sum(x)) - _TARGET_SUM


def full_g_values(h, n):
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full") * dx


def exact_c5(h, n):
    return float(np.max(full_g_values(h, n)))


def project_feasible(h, n):
    target = 0.5 * float(n)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)

    for _ in range(300):
        s = float(np.sum(h))
        diff = target - s
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            room = 1.0 - h
            r = float(np.sum(room))
            if r < 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.minimum(1.0, h + (diff / r) * room)
        else:
            s2 = s
            if s2 < 1e-15:
                break
            h = np.maximum(0.0, h + (diff / s2) * h)

    diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
    if abs(diff) > 1e-12:
        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = h < (1.0 - 1e-14)
        else:
            mask = h > 1e-14
        if np.any(mask):
            h[mask] += diff / float(np.sum(mask))
            np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    return h


def strict_project_feasible(h, n):
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    target = 0.5 * float(n)

    for _ in range(3000):
        diff = target - float(np.sum(h))
        if abs(diff) < 1e-14:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            idx = int(np.argmax(1.0 - h))
            step = min(diff, 1.0 - h[idx])
            if step <= 0.0:
                break
            h[idx] += step
        else:
            idx = int(np.argmin(h))
            step = min(-diff, h[idx])
            if step <= 0.0:
                break
            h[idx] -= step
    return h


def interpolate_to(h, n_old, n_new):
    n_old = int(n_old)
    n_new = int(n_new)
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    if n_old <= 1:
        return np.full(n_new, float(np.mean(h)), dtype=float)
    if n_new <= 1:
        return np.array([float(np.mean(h))], dtype=float)
    if n_old == n_new:
        return h.copy()

    oldx = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_old)
    newx = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n_new)
    return np.interp(newx, oldx, h)


def make_tent_start(n):
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float)
    y = 2.0 * np.arange(n, dtype=float) / max(1, n - 1) - 1.0
    f = 1.0 - 2.0 * np.abs(y)
    h = 0.5 * (1.0 + f)
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def make_random_start(n, seed):
    n = int(n)
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    if n <= 1:
        return np.full(n, 0.5, dtype=float)
    y = 2.0 * np.arange(n, dtype=float) / max(1, n - 1) - 1.0

    f = 0.75 * (1.0 - 2.0 * np.abs(y))
    for k in range(1, 7):
        amp = math.exp(-0.22 * (k - 1))
        f += rng.normal(0.0, amp) * np.cos(k * math.pi * y)

    h = 0.5 * (1.0 + f)
    return project_feasible(h, n)


def add_lag_grad(grad, d, w, x, n):
    if n <= 0 or w == 0.0:
        return
    d = int(d)
    if d == 0:
        grad[:] += w
        grad[:] -= 2.0 * w * x
    elif d > 0:
        L = n - d
        if L <= 0:
            return
        grad[:L] += w
        grad[:L] -= w * x[d:d + L]
        grad[d:] -= w * x[:L]
    else:
        e = -d
        L = n - e
        if L <= 0:
            return
        grad[e:] += w
        grad[e:] -= w * x[:L]
        grad[:L] -= w * x[e:]


def active_objective(x, n, dlist, k):
    n = int(n)
    k = int(k)
    if k <= 0 or dlist is None or len(dlist) == 0:
        return 0.0
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    total = 0.0

    for dd in dlist:
        d = int(dd)
        if d == 0:
            total += float(np.sum(x) - np.dot(x, x))
        elif d > 0:
            L = n - d
            if L > 0:
                total += float(np.sum(x[:L]) - np.dot(x[:L], x[d:d + L]))
        else:
            e = -d
            L = n - e
            if L > 0:
                total += float(np.sum(x[e:]) - np.dot(x[e:], x[:L]))

    return (total * dx) / float(k)


def active_gradient(x, n, dlist, k):
    n = int(n)
    k = int(k)
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if k <= 0 or dlist is None or len(dlist) == 0:
        return grad

    w = (2.0 / float(n)) / float(k)
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=float)

    for dd in dlist:
        d = int(dd)
        if d == 0:
            grad[:] += w
            grad[:] -= 2.0 * w * x
        elif d > 0:
            L = n - d
            if L > 0:
                grad[:L] += w
                grad[:L] -= w * x[d:d + L]
                grad[d:] -= w * x[:L]
        else:
            e = -d
            L = n - e
            if L > 0:
                grad[e:] += w
                grad[e:] -= w * x[:L]
                grad[:L] -= w * x[e:]

    return grad


def choose_active_d(vals, n, maxK, tol):
    n = int(n)
    maxK = int(maxK)
    if maxK < 1:
        maxK = 1
    if maxK > len(vals):
        maxK = len(vals)

    m = float(np.max(vals))
    idx = np.where(vals >= m - max(1e-12, tol))[0]
    if idx.size == 0:
        idx = np.array([int(np.argmax(vals))])

    if idx.size > maxK:
        idx = idx[np.argsort(vals[idx])[::-1][:maxK]]

    dlist = (n - 1) - idx
    return np.asarray(dlist, dtype=int)


def run_slsqp(func, x0, jac, args, n, maxiter):
    global _TARGET_SUM
    n = int(n)
    _TARGET_SUM = 0.5 * float(n)

    x0 = np.asarray(x0, dtype=float)
    bounds = [(0.0, 1.0)] * n
    cons = [{"type": "eq", "fun": _sum_constraint}]

    try:
        res = minimize(
            func,
            x0,
            method="SLSQP",
            bounds=bounds,
            constraints=cons,
            jac=jac,
            args=args,
            options={"maxiter": int(maxiter), "ftol": 1e-12}
        )
        return np.asarray(res.x, dtype=float)
    except Exception:
        return x0.copy()


def try_candidate(x, n, best_h, best_c):
    p = project_feasible(x, n)
    c = exact_c5(p, n)
    if c < best_c - 1e-14:
        return p, c, True
    return best_h, best_c, False


def active_polish(h, n, deadline, rng):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h
    best_c = exact_c5(best_h, n)

    maxK = max(16, min(320, 32 + n // 2))
    Ks = []
    for K in (8, 16, 32, 64, 128, 256, 384, 512):
        if K == 0:
            continue
        if K > len(full_g_values(np.zeros(n), n)) - 1:
            K = len(full_g_values(np.zeros(n), n)) - 1
        if K > maxK:
            continue
        if K not in Ks:
            Ks.append(K)

    tol_list = (1e-3, 4e-4, 1.5e-4, 5e-5, 1.5e-5, 4e-6)

    if n <= 350:
        max_calls = 80
    elif n <= 700:
        max_calls = 40
    else:
        max_calls = 22

    calls = 0

    for K in Ks:
        if calls >= max_calls:
            break
        for tol in tol_list:
            if calls >= max_calls:
                break
            if time.time() >= deadline - 25.0:
                return best_h, best_c

            subs = 0
            improved_cycle = False

            while subs < 3 and calls < max_calls and time.time() < deadline - 25.0:
                vals = full_g_values(best_h, n)
                dlist = choose_active_d(vals, n, K, tol)
                if dlist is None or len(dlist) == 0:
                    break

                hstart = best_h
                if subs > 0:
                    noise_scale = 0.008 + 0.012 * float(subs)
                    hstart = project_feasible(best_h + noise_scale * rng.randn(n), n)

                if K <= 16:
                    mi = 520
                elif K <= 64:
                    mi = 380
                elif K <= 128:
                    mi = 260
                else:
                    mi = 160

                left = max(0.0, deadline - time.time())
                if left < 80.0:
                    mi = int(max(50, min(mi, int(left))))

                res = run_slsqp(
                    active_objective,
                    hstart,
                    active_gradient,
                    (n, dlist, int(len(dlist))),
                    n,
                    mi
                )
                calls += 1

                improved = False
                p, c, ok = try_candidate(res, n, best_h, best_c)
                if ok:
                    best_h = p
                    best_c = c
                    improved = True

                if not improved:
                    for f in (0.5, 0.25, 0.75):
                        cand = best_h + f * (res - best_h)
                        p, c, ok = try_candidate(cand, n, best_h, best_c)
                        if ok:
                            best_h = p
                            best_c = c
                            improved = True
                            break

                if improved:
                    improved_cycle = True
                    subs += 1
                else:
                    break

            if not improved_cycle and time.time() < deadline - 60.0 and calls < max_calls:
                hstart = project_feasible(best_h + 0.05 * rng.randn(n), n)
                vals = full_g_values(hstart, n)
                dlist = choose_active_d(vals, n, K, tol)
                if dlist is not None and len(dlist) > 0:
                    res = run_slsqp(
                        active_objective,
                        hstart,
                        active_gradient,
                        (n, dlist, int(len(dlist))),
                        n,
                        220
                    )
                    calls += 1
                    best_h, best_c, _ = try_candidate(res, n, best_h, best_c)

    return best_h, best_c


def smooth_objective(x, n, alpha):
    g = full_g_values(x, n)
    m = float(np.max(g))
    if alpha <= 0.0:
        return m
    z = float(alpha) * (g - m)
    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    z = z - zmax
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0:
        return m
    return m + (zmax + math.log(s)) / float(alpha)


def smooth_gradient(x, n, alpha):
    g = full_g_values(x, n)
    m = float(np.max(g))
    z = float(alpha) * (g - m)
    zmax = float(np.max(z))
    z = z - zmax
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(np.sum(e))
    if s <= 0.0:
        return np.zeros(n, dtype=float)

    p = e / s
    grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    dx = 2.0 / float(n)
    for i, pd in enumerate(p):
        if pd > 1e-10:
            d = (n - 1) - i
            add_lag_grad(grad, d, pd * dx, x, n)
    return grad


def smooth_polish(h, n, deadline, rng):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h
    best_c = exact_c5(best_h, n)

    alphas = (80.0, 250.0, 800.0, 2500.0)
    max_calls = 12

    calls = 0
    for alpha in alphas:
        if calls >= max_calls or time.time() >= deadline - 40.0:
            break

        for attempt in range(2):
            if calls >= max_calls or time.time() >= deadline - 40.0:
                break

            hstart = best_h
            if attempt == 1:
                hstart = project_feasible(best_h + 0.03 * rng.randn(n), n)

            mi = 320 if alpha <= 300.0 else 200
            left = max(0.0, deadline - time.time())
            if left < 90.0:
                mi = int(max(50, min(mi, int(left))))

            res = run_slsqp(
                smooth_objective,
                hstart,
                smooth_gradient,
                (n, alpha),
                n,
                mi
            )
            calls += 1

            _, _, ok = try_candidate(res, n, best_h, best_c)
            if ok:
                continue

            for f in (0.5, 0.25, 0.75):
                cand = best_h + f * (res - best_h)
                best_h, best_c, ok = try_candidate(cand, n, best_h, best_c)
                if ok:
                    break

    return best_h, best_c


def local_polish(h, n, deadline, rng):
    n = int(n)
    h = project_feasible(h, n)
    best_h = h
    best_c = exact_c5(best_h, n)

    if n <= 350:
        max_iters = 2600
    elif n <= 700:
        max_iters = 1200
    else:
        max_iters = 500

    step = 0.025
    it = 0

    while it < max_iters and time.time() < deadline - 20.0:
        it += 1
        x = best_h.copy()

        if rng.rand() < 0.55:
            i = int(rng.randint(n))
            j = int(rng.randint(n))
            if i == j:
                continue
            delta = step * (2.0 * rng.rand() - 1.0)
            ni = x[i] + delta
            nj = x[j] - delta
            if ni < 0.0 or ni > 1.0 or nj < 0.0 or nj > 1.0:
                step = max(0.004, step * 0.97)
                continue
            x[i] = ni
            x[j] = nj
        else:
            noise = rng.normal(0.0, step, n)
            noise -= float(np.mean(noise))
            x = project_feasible(best_h + noise, n)

        c = exact_c5(x, n)
        if c < best_c - 1e-13:
            best_h = x
            best_c = c
            step = min(0.10, step * 1.18)
        else:
            step = max(0.004, step * 0.985)

    return best_h, best_c


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    budget = max(60.0, float(budget_s))
    deadline = t0 + budget - 8.0
    rng = np.random.RandomState(int(seed) if seed is not None else 42)

    h_init = None
    try:
        _ihv = initial_h_values
        if _ihv is not None:
            h_init = np.asarray(_ihv, dtype=float)
    except Exception:
        h_init = None

    if h_init is None or h_init.size == 0:
        n_base = 250
        best_h = make_tent_start(n_base)
    else:
        n0 = int(h_init.size)
        if n0 > 600:
            n_base = 512
            best_h = interpolate_to(h_init, n0, n_base)
        elif n0 < 150:
            n_base = 250
            best_h = interpolate_to(h_init, n0, n_base)
        else:
            n_base = n0
            best_h = h_init.copy()
        best_h = project_feasible(best_h, n_base)

    best_n = int(n_base)
    best_c = exact_c5(best_h, best_n)

    if time.time() < t0 + 30.0:
        candidates = [best_h]
        candidates.append(best_h + best_h[::-1])
        candidates.append(make_tent_start(best_n))
        for s in range(5):
            candidates.append(make_random_start(best_n, 100 + s))

        for cand in candidates:
            p = project_feasible(cand, best_n)
            c = exact_c5(p, best_n)
            if c < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_h = p
                best_c = c

    t1 = min(deadline, t0 + 0.46 * budget)
    best_h, best_c = active_polish(best_h, best_n, t1, rng)
    best_n = int(best_n)

    t2 = min(deadline, t0 + 0.63 * budget)
    if time.time() < t2 - 20.0:
        best_h, best_c = smooth_polish(best_h, best_n, t2, rng)

    t3 = min(deadline, t0 + 0.72 * budget)
    if time.time() < t3 - 20.0:
        best_h, best_c = local_polish(best_h, best_n, t3, rng)

    if time.time() < deadline - 120.0 and best_n <= 300:
        n2 = min(max(512, 2 * best_n), 600)
        if n2 > best_n:
            h2 = project_feasible(interpolate_to(best_h, best_n, n2), n2)
            c2 = exact_c5(h2, n2)
            hr_deadline = min(deadline, t0 + 0.97 * budget)
            h2, c2 = active_polish(h2, n2, hr_deadline, rng)
            if c2 < best_c - 1e-14:
                best_h = h2
                best_c = c2
                best_n = int(n2)

    if time.time() < deadline - 35.0:
        best_h, best_c = local_polish(best_h, best_n, deadline - 25.0, rng)

    best_h = strict_project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
    c_final = exact_c5(best_h, best_n)
    if c_final > best_c + 1e-12:
        h_alt = project_feasible(best_h, best_n)
        c_alt = exact_c5(h_alt, best_n)
        if c_alt <= best_c + 1e-12:
            best_h = h_alt
            c_final = c_alt

    return best_h, float(c_final), int(best_n)
```
